I am writing concerning your request for information regarding information on any UFO sightings
reported to the MOD on or around 5 May 1990 in the UK, specifically the Somerset area. Your
request has been passed to this Department as we are the focal point within the Ministry of
Defence for correspondence regarding UFOs. Sorry for the long delay in getting back to you.

I have looked through our records from on and around the 5 May 1990 and confirm there were no
sightings reported for that date from anywhere in the United Kingdom. I have, however, found 5
reports around that date, (not from Somerset) and I enclose copies of these for your information.

If this information does not address your requirements or you wish to complain about any aspect
of the handling of this request, then you should contact the undersigned in the first instance.
Should you remain dissatisfied, then you may apply for an internal review by contacting the
Director of Information Exploitation, 6" Floor, MOD Main Building, Whitehall, SW1A 2HB. If
you are still unhappy following an internal review, you may wish to take your complaint to the
Information Commissioner under the provisions of Section 50 of the Freedom of Information Act.
Please note that the Information Commissioner will not investigate your case until the MOD
internal review process has been completed. Further details of the role and powers of the
Information Commissioner can be found on the Commissioner’s website,

http://www.informationcommissioner.gov.uk.
Hope these will be helpful.

Yours sincerely

AUTHORISATION FOR THE RELEASE OF INFORMATION

Case Number: 30-06-2005-105528-004 Expiry: 27/07/2005

The Applicant has made the following request for information:

HE sic he would like to see the Ministry of Defence logs of UFO
activity in Sussex over the last 20 years.

Case for release of information

BE has been informed that we do not or never have kept log

books of any UFO activity that has happened in the United Kingdom.
Have informed him as to where there is information on UFQs that he can
view.

Have also given him UFO sightings from the years 2002 to 2005, for his
information.

Authorisation

| hereby give authorisation for the release of the aforementioned information
to the Applicant.

Grade/Rank: ot ce deeueneeeaeees — | Veueeeeeeeseeaenes
lam writing concerning your Freedom of Information request for Defence logs of UFO activity
in Sussex over the last 20 years. Your request has been passed to this Department as we are
the focal point within the Ministry of Defence for correspondence regarding UFOs. | apologise
for the delay in replying.

The MOD does not keep logs recording UFO sightings. With regard to the records we hold of
UFO sightings reported to the MOD | should inform you that they are not held electronically
but are filed on paper files in the order in which they are received and we currently hold such
records spanning a 25 year period. Before any of this information can be released, personal
data has to be removed in accordance with the Data Protection Act 1998. If we were to
process all the reports you requested, the costs would exceed the permitted £600 limit set for
compliance with the Freedom of Information Act 2000. However, we are currently in the
process of compiling a database of UFO reports. This has now been completed for the years,
2002, 2003, 2004 and to date in 2005. | have looked through these records and found 7
reports from those years.

The following table shows the relevan(=yting reports that were found for Sussex for the
years - 2002, 2003 and 2005.

15/05/02 02.15 Uckfield East Sussex | One object with red and
green flashing lights.

16/08/02 20.12 Brighton East Sussex | Big flying object, looked like a
glider.

22/08/02 22.25 Heathfield East Sussex | Aerial lights seen in the sky.

05/09/02 Wisborough West Interesting lights on an

Green Sussex equilateral triangle.

19/12/02 06.30 Brighton East Sussex | Large cylindrical object with a
white flowing light from the
front and flames coming from
the rear.

[22/09/03 | [| Hove | East Sussex | A red UFO. ]

03/08/05 00.00 Peacehaven East Sussex | There were seven red and

white flashing lights. They
were moving in an erratic
type of way.

You may also wish to be aware that there is already some information about UFOs available
for public viewing. MOD files were routinely destroyed after 5 years until 1967 when they were
generally preserved for The National Archives. A few have survived before 1967 and these
together with records up to 1977 are now available for viewing. The National Archives can be
contacted at Ruskin Avenue, Kew, Richmond, Surrey TW9 4DU or telephone, 020 8876 3444.
The National Archives also have a website giving information about the records they hold and
how to access them. This can be found on the internet at http://Awww.nationalarchives.gov.uk.
The Ministry of Defence Freedom of information Publication Scheme also contains
information on UFOs which can be accessed via the internet at http://www.foi.mod.uk. A
search in the Scheme under ‘UFO’ will take you to this information. Further information can
also be found in the Publication Scheme Reading Room.

\f this information does not address your requirements or you wish to complain about any
aspect of the handling of this request, then you should contact the undersigned in the first
instance. Should you remain dissatisfied, then you may apply for an internal review by
contacting the Director of Information Exploitation, 6" Floor, MOD Main Building, Whitehall,
SW1A 2HB.
If you are still unhappy following an internal review, you may wish to take your complaint to
the Information Commissioner under the provisions of Section 50 of the Freedom of
Information Act. Please note that the Information Commissioner will not investigate your case
until the MOD internal review process has been completed. Further details of the role and
powers of the Information Commissioner can be found on the Commissioner’s website,

http://www. informationcommissioner.gov.uk.

This office is the focal point for UFO sighting reports and correspondence for the whole of the Ministry of
Defence and any reports made to RAF Stations are forwarded to us.

Fit Lt <0 psdAt|me your last message, so | expect he will also be forwarding your letter to me when he

mod.uk]

With regard to your request for information it may be helpful if 1 explain that the we receive reports of UFO
sightings from all over the UK and we currently hold records of reports received from 1978 to date . These
reports are not held electronically or segregated by location, but are filed on paper files in the order in which
they were received. This means that in order to locate reports from specific areas (even for a limited period) it
is necessary to conduct a manual search of hundreds of individual records and often the costs of such an
excess exceeds the permitted £600 limit set for compliance with the Freedom of Information Act 2000.
However, in order to assist members of the public we are compiling a database of the UFO sighting reports
we hold, which we intend to publish in the near future. While we have not completed these records for as far
back as 2000 we have completed them for the years 2002, 2003, 2004 and 2005 (to date) and if you would
like to inform me what particular area you are interested in, | will search these records for anything relevant. |
see in your e-mail that you say "the area covered by Lyneham". RAF Lyneham aircraft fly all over the UK (and
abroad), but perhaps you mean in the immediate vicinity of RAF Lyneham, the town of Lyneham or perhaps
Wiltshire?

If you could clarify, | will see what information we hold.

Yours sincerely,

— of Air Staff - Freedom of Information
